## How to use in another tool-enabled chat

1. Copy only the Base64 block below, excluding the backticks.
2. Save it as `soul_land_4_fire_phoenix_foundation.zip.b64`.
3. Decode it back into a ZIP:

```bash
base64 -d soul_land_4_fire_phoenix_foundation.zip.b64 > soul_land_4_fire_phoenix_foundation.zip
sha256sum soul_land_4_fire_phoenix_foundation.zip
unzip -t soul_land_4_fire_phoenix_foundation.zip
```

4. The SHA256 must match `c5b0beb92493bf11c5ec386d863e14397485df69f972dfe54801b95d83a89cfb`.

If the new chat cannot decode Base64 or handle binary files, use `/home/user/SOUL_LAND_4_FIRE_PHOENIX_COMPLETE_NEW_CHAT_HANDOFF.md` instead. That handoff contains the full text of all project Markdown files and is enough to continue writing.
